import io
import math
import os
import re
import uuid
import base64
import string
import threading
import requests
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import cv2
import fitz
import numpy as np
from paddleocr import PaddleOCR

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
from flask import Flask, jsonify, request, send_file, session

app = Flask(__name__, static_folder="public", static_url_path="")

app.secret_key = os.getenv("SECRET_KEY", "my_super_secret_key")
app.config["MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH"] = 50 * 1024 * 1024

ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTS = {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp", ".bmp", ".tif", ".tiff"}

OCR_ENGINE = PaddleOCR(use_textline_orientation=True, lang="en")
FONT_TELUGU = "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/lohit-telugu/Lohit-Telugu.ttf"
ADMIN_PIN = os.environ.get("ADMIN_PIN", "0000")
VAULT_PIN = os.environ.get("VAULT_PIN", "1111")
SESSIONS = {}

def get_ist_time():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)


@app.get("/")
def index():
    return app.send_static_file("index.html")


def contains_telugu(text: str) -> bool:
    return any("\u0c00" <= c <= "\u0c7f" for c in text)


def get_ocr_data(bgr_img: np.ndarray):
    rgb = cv2.cvtColor(bgr_img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
    result = OCR_ENGINE.ocr(rgb, cls=True)
    data = {"text": [], "conf": [], "left": [], "top": [], "width": [], "height": []}

    if not result:
        return data, 1.0

    for line in result:
        if not line:
            continue
        for item in line:
            box = item[0]
            text = item[1][0]
            conf = float(item[1][1])

            xs = [p[0] for p in box]
            ys = [p[1] for p in box]

            x, y = min(xs), min(ys)
            w, h = max(xs) - x, max(ys) - y

            data["text"].append(text)
            data["conf"].append(conf * 100)
            data["left"].append(int(x))
            data["top"].append(int(y))
            data["width"].append(int(w))
            data["height"].append(int(h))

    return data, 1.0


def inpaint_image_patch(roi_bgr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    if roi_bgr.size == 0:
        return roi_bgr
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(roi_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    mask = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(gray, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_MEAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, 15, 12)
    mask[0:2, :], mask[-2:, :], mask[:, 0:2], mask[:, -2:] = 0, 0, 0, 0
    mask = cv2.dilate(mask, np.ones((3, 3), np.uint8), iterations=1)
    return cv2.inpaint(roi_bgr, mask, 3, cv2.INPAINT_TELEA)


def extract_dynamic_color(bgr_patch: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
    if bgr_patch.size == 0:
        return (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(bgr_patch, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    mask = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(gray, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_MEAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, 15, 12)
    text_pixels = bgr_patch[mask == 255]
    if len(text_pixels) > 0:
        mc = np.median(text_pixels, axis=0)
        return (float(mc[2]) / 255.0, float(mc[1]) / 255.0, float(mc[0]) / 255.0)
    return (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)


def get_github_headers():
    token = os.environ.get("GITHUB_TOKEN")
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "Accept": "application/vnd.github.v3+json"} if token else None


def get_github_repo():
    return os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPO", "photo-storage")


def backup_to_github(file_bytes: bytes, original_filename: str, state_folder: str):
    headers, repo = get_github_headers(), get_github_repo()
    branch = os.environ.get("GITHUB_BRANCH", "main")
    if not headers or not repo:
        return

    def task():
        try:
            timestamp = get_ist_time().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
            ext = Path(original_filename).suffix.lower()
            stem = Path(original_filename).stem
            # Changed format to filename_timestamp
            safe_filename = f"{stem}_{timestamp}_edited{ext}" if state_folder == "Downloaded" else f"{stem}_{timestamp}{ext}"
            file_type = "PDFs" if ext == ".pdf" else "Images"
            path = f"Editing/{state_folder}/{file_type}/{safe_filename}"
            requests.put(
                f"https://api.github.com/repos/{repo}/contents/{path}",
                json={"message": f"Auto-backup: {path}", "content": base64.b64encode(file_bytes).decode("utf-8"), "branch": branch},
                headers=headers,
                timeout=30,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            app.logger.error(f"GitHub Backup Failed: {str(e)}")

    threading.Thread(target=task, daemon=True).start()

def is_admin():
    return session.get("admin", False)

@app.post("/api/admin/verify")
def verify_admin():
    if request.json.get("pin") == ADMIN_PIN:
        session["admin"] = True
        return jsonify({"status": "success"})
    return jsonify({"error": "Unauthorized"}), 401


@app.post("/api/admin/verify_vault")
def verify_vault():
    if not is_admin():
        return jsonify({"error": "Unauthorized"}), 401
    if request.json.get("pin") == VAULT_PIN:
        return jsonify({"status": "success"})
    return jsonify({"error": "Unauthorized"}), 401

@app.get("/api/admin/list")
def admin_list():
    if not is_admin():
        return jsonify({"error": "Unauthorized"}), 401
    headers, repo = get_github_headers(), get_github_repo()
    if not headers:
        return jsonify({"error": "Admin not configured"}), 403
    url = f"https://api.github.com/repos/{repo}/contents/Editing/{request.args.get('folder')}/{request.args.get('type')}"
    resp = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    if resp.status_code == 404:
        return jsonify([])
    elif resp.status_code != 200:
        return jsonify({"error": "GitHub API error"}), 500
    return jsonify([{"name": f["name"], "path": f["path"], "sha": f["sha"], "url": f["download_url"]} for f in resp.json() if f["type"] == "file"])


@app.post("/api/admin/action")
def admin_action():
    if not is_admin():
        return jsonify({"error": "Unauthorized"}), 401
    action, files = request.json.get("action"), request.json.get("files", [])
    headers, repo = get_github_headers(), get_github_repo()
    if not headers:
        return jsonify({"error": "Unauthorized"}), 403
    
    for f in files:
        target_url = f"https://api.github.com/repos/{repo}/contents/{f['path']}"
        if action == "trash":
            get_resp = requests.get(target_url, headers=headers)
            if get_resp.status_code == 200:
                new_path = f["path"].replace("Uploaded", "Deleted").replace("Downloaded", "Deleted")
                requests.put(
                    f"https://api.github.com/repos/{repo}/contents/{new_path}",
                    headers=headers,
                    json={"message": "Moved to trash", "content": get_resp.json()["content"], "branch": os.environ.get("GITHUB_BRANCH", "main")},
                )
        requests.delete(target_url, headers=headers, json={"message": "Deleted file", "sha": f["sha"], "branch": os.environ.get("GITHUB_BRANCH", "main")})
    return jsonify({"status": "success"})


@app.post("/api/extract")
def extract_region():
    data = request.json
    sid, box = data.get("session_id"), data.get("box")
    if sid not in SESSIONS:
        return jsonify({"error": "Session expired."}), 404
    
    sess = SESSIONS[sid]
    extracted_lines = []
    default_size, dominant_angle, max_area = 12.0, 0, 0
    is_bold, dominant_color = False, (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)

    if sess["ext"] == "pdf":
        doc = fitz.open(stream=sess["history"][sess["current_index"]], filetype="pdf")
        page = doc[sess["current_page"]]
        rect = fitz.Rect(box[0], box[1], box[2], box[3])
        for block in page.get_text("dict", clip=rect).get("blocks", []):
            if block.get("type") != 0:
                continue
            for line in block.get("lines", []):
                words = []
                for span in line.get("spans", []):
                    if text := span.get("text", "").strip():
                        words.append(text)
                        intersect = fitz.Rect(span["bbox"]).intersect(rect).get_area()
                        if intersect > max_area:
                            max_area = intersect
                            default_size = span.get("size", default_size)
                            dominant_angle = (int(round(math.degrees(math.atan2(-line.get("dir", (1.0, 0.0))[1], line.get("dir", (1.0, 0.0))[0])) / 90.0) * 90) % 360)
                            is_bold = bool(span.get("flags", 0) & 18 or "bold" in span.get("font", "").lower() or "black" in span.get("font", "").lower())
                            c = (((span.get("color", 0) >> 16) & 255) / 255.0, ((span.get("color", 0) >> 8) & 255) / 255.0, (span.get("color", 0) & 255) / 255.0)
                            dominant_color = c if sum(c) <= 2.8 else (0.0, 0.0, 0.0)
                if words:
                    extracted_lines.append({"text": " ".join(words), "word_count": len(words), "char_count": sum(len(w) for w in words)})
        doc.close()
    else:
        img = cv2.imdecode(np.frombuffer(sess["history"][sess["current_index"]], dtype=np.uint8), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = [int(v) for v in box]
        roi = img[max(0, y0):min(img.shape[0], y1), max(0, x0):min(img.shape[1], x1)]
        if roi.size > 0:
            data_ocr, _ = get_ocr_data(roi)
            words = [data_ocr["text"][i].strip() for i in range(len(data_ocr["text"])) if data_ocr["text"][i].strip() and float(data_ocr["conf"][i]) > 15.0]
            if words:
                extracted_lines.append({"text": " ".join(words), "word_count": len(words), "char_count": sum(len(w) for w in words)})
            dominant_color = extract_dynamic_color(roi)

    return jsonify({"lines": extracted_lines, "suggested_font_size": default_size, "angle": dominant_angle, "is_bold": is_bold, "color": dominant_color})


@app.post("/api/upload")
def upload_file():
    if "file" not in request.files:
        return jsonify({"error": "No file uploaded."}), 400
    uploaded = request.files["file"]
    raw_bytes = uploaded.read()
    backup_to_github(raw_bytes, uploaded.filename, "Uploaded")
    ext = Path(uploaded.filename).suffix.lower()
    
    if ext == ".pdf":
        doc = fitz.open(stream=raw_bytes, filetype="pdf")
        img_bytes = doc[0].get_pixmap(dpi=150).tobytes("png")
        width, height = doc[0].rect.width, doc[0].rect.height
        total_pages = len(doc)
        doc.close()
    else:
        img = cv2.imdecode(np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=np.uint8), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
        height, width = img.shape[:2]
        img_bytes = bytes(cv2.imencode(".png", img)[1])
        total_pages = 1

    sid = str(uuid.uuid4())
    SESSIONS[sid] = {
        "history": [raw_bytes],
        "preview_history": [img_bytes],
        "current_index": 0,
        "current_page": 0,
        "total_pages": total_pages,
        "ext": "pdf" if ext == ".pdf" else "img",
        "orig_ext": ext,
        "filename": uploaded.filename,
        "width": width,
        "height": height,
    }
    
    return jsonify({
        "session_id": sid,
        "width": width,
        "height": height,
        "filename": uploaded.filename,
        "total_pages": total_pages,
        "current_page": 1,
    })


@app.get("/api/preview/<sid>")
def get_preview(sid):
    if sid not in SESSIONS:
        return jsonify({"error": "Not found"}), 404
    s = SESSIONS[sid]
    
    if s["ext"] != "pdf":
        return send_file(io.BytesIO(s["preview_history"][s["current_index"]]), mimetype="image/png")
    
    doc = fitz.open(stream=s["history"][s["current_index"]], filetype="pdf")
    page = doc[s["current_page"]]
    png = page.get_pixmap(dpi=150).tobytes("png")
    doc.close()
    return send_file(io.BytesIO(png), mimetype="image/png")


@app.post("/api/page")
def change_page():
    sid = request.json.get("session_id")
    action = request.json.get("action")
    if sid not in SESSIONS:
        return jsonify({"error": "Session expired"}), 404
    
    s = SESSIONS[sid]
    if action == "next":
        s["current_page"] = min(s["current_page"] + 1, s["total_pages"] - 1)
    elif action == "prev":
        s["current_page"] = max(s["current_page"] - 1, 0)
        
    width, height = s["width"], s["height"]
    if s["ext"] == "pdf":
        doc = fitz.open(stream=s["history"][s["current_index"]], filetype="pdf")
        page = doc[s["current_page"]]
        width, height = page.rect.width, page.rect.height
        s["width"], s["height"] = width, height
        doc.close()
        
    return jsonify({"page": s["current_page"] + 1, "total_pages": s["total_pages"], "width": width, "height": height})


@app.post("/api/history")
def handle_history():
    sid, action = request.json.get("session_id"), request.json.get("action")
    if sid not in SESSIONS:
        return jsonify({"error": "Session expired."}), 404
    sess = SESSIONS[sid]
    
    if action == "undo" and sess["current_index"] > 0:
        sess["current_index"] -= 1
    elif action == "redo" and sess["current_index"] < len(sess["history"]) - 1:
        sess["current_index"] += 1
    else:
        return jsonify({"error": "Cannot perform history action."}), 400
        
    return jsonify({"status": "success", "can_undo": sess["current_index"] > 0, "can_redo": sess["current_index"] < len(sess["history"]) - 1})


@app.post("/api/edit")
def apply_edits():
    sid = request.json.get("session_id")
    is_preview = request.json.get("is_preview", False)
    if sid not in SESSIONS:
        return jsonify({"error": "Session expired."}), 404
    sess = SESSIONS[sid]
    
    try:
        if sess["ext"] == "pdf":
            updated_bytes = apply_pdf_edits(sess["history"][sess["current_index"]], request.json.get("edits", []), request.json.get("mode"), sess["current_page"])
            doc = fitz.open(stream=updated_bytes, filetype="pdf")
            preview_bytes = doc[sess["current_page"]].get_pixmap(dpi=150).tobytes("png")
            doc.close()
        else:
            updated_img = apply_image_edits(cv2.imdecode(np.frombuffer(sess["history"][sess["current_index"]], dtype=np.uint8), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR), request.json.get("edits", []), request.json.get("mode"))
            updated_bytes = bytes(cv2.imencode(sess["orig_ext"] if sess["orig_ext"] in ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTS else ".png", updated_img)[1])
            preview_bytes = bytes(cv2.imencode(".png", updated_img)[1])
            
        if is_preview:
            return jsonify({"status": "preview", "image": base64.b64encode(preview_bytes).decode("utf-8")})
            
        sess["history"] = sess["history"][:sess["current_index"] + 1] + [updated_bytes]
        sess["preview_history"] = sess["preview_history"][:sess["current_index"] + 1] + [preview_bytes]
        sess["current_index"] += 1
        return jsonify({"status": "success", "can_undo": True, "can_redo": False})
    except Exception as e:
        app.logger.exception("Edit failed")
        return jsonify({"error": str(e)}), 500


@app.get("/api/download/<sid>")
def download_file(sid):
    if sid not in SESSIONS:
        return jsonify({"error": "Not found"}), 404
    sess = SESSIONS[sid]
    current_bytes = sess["history"][sess["current_index"]]
    # Changed format to filename_timestamp for final download as well
    out_name = f"{Path(sess['filename']).stem}_{get_ist_time().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}_edited{sess['orig_ext']}"
    backup_to_github(current_bytes, sess["filename"], "Downloaded")
    return send_file(io.BytesIO(current_bytes), mimetype=("application/pdf" if sess["ext"] == "pdf" else f"image/{sess['orig_ext'].replace('.','')}"), download_name=out_name, as_attachment=True)


@app.post("/api/admin/logout")
def admin_logout():
    session.pop("admin", None)
    return jsonify({"status": "success"})


def extract_character_sequence(page: fitz.Page) -> List[dict]:
    char_list = []
    for block in page.get_text("rawdict").get("blocks", []):
        if block.get("type") != 0:
            continue
        for line in block.get("lines", []):
            dir_vec = line.get("dir", (1.0, 0.0))
            for span in line.get("spans", []):
                for char in span.get("chars", []):
                    if not char.get("c", "").strip():
                        continue
                    char_list.append({"text": char["c"], "bbox": fitz.Rect(char["bbox"]), "origin": char["origin"], "dir": dir_vec, "size": span.get("size", 10.0), "flags": span.get("flags", 0), "font": span.get("font", "helv"), "color": span.get("color", 0)})
    return char_list


def find_matches_via_matrix(char_list: List[dict], search_str: str) -> List[dict]:
    target = search_str.strip()
    if not target:
        return []
    full_text, mapping = "", []
    
    for i, char_data in enumerate(char_list):
        if i > 0:
            prev = char_list[i - 1]
            if abs(char_data["bbox"].y0 - prev["bbox"].y0) < char_data["size"] * 0.5:
                if char_data["bbox"].x0 - prev["bbox"].x1 > char_data["size"] * 0.35:
                    full_text += " "
                    mapping.append(i)
            else:
                full_text += "\n"
                mapping.append(i)
        for _ in range(len(char_data["text"])):
            mapping.append(i)
        full_text += char_data["text"]
        
    matches, start_idx = [], 0
    while True:
        idx = full_text.find(target, start_idx)
        if idx == -1:
            break
        prev_char = full_text[idx - 1] if idx > 0 else " "
        next_char = full_text[idx + len(target)] if idx + len(target) < len(full_text) else " "
        if not prev_char.isalnum() and not next_char.isalnum():
            match_chars = char_list[mapping[idx] : mapping[idx + len(target) - 1] + 1]
            combined_rect = fitz.Rect(match_chars[0]["bbox"])
            for c in match_chars[1:]:
                combined_rect |= c["bbox"]
            matches.append({"rect": combined_rect, "first_char": match_chars[0]})
        start_idx = idx + 1
    return matches


def apply_pdf_edits(raw_bytes: bytes, edits: list, mode: str, page_index: int = 0) -> bytes:
    doc = fitz.open(stream=raw_bytes, filetype="pdf")
    page = doc[page_index]
    masks, inserts = [], []

    if mode == "manual":
        for edit in edits:
            rect = fitz.Rect(*edit["box"])
            search_str = edit.get("search", "").strip()
            ox, oy = float(edit.get("offset_x", 0)), float(edit.get("offset_y", 0))
            osz, obold = float(edit.get("offset_size", 0)), int(edit.get("offset_bold", 0))
            col = tuple(edit.get("color", [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]))

            if search_str and edit.get("type") == "text":
                box_chars = [c for c in extract_character_sequence(page) if rect.intersects(c["bbox"])]
                matches = find_matches_via_matrix(box_chars, search_str)
                if matches:
                    for match in matches:
                        r, fc = match["rect"], match["first_char"]
                        masks.append(fitz.Rect(r.x0 - 1.5, r.y0 - 1.5, r.x1 + 1.5, r.y1 + 1.5))
                        tel = contains_telugu(edit["replace"])
                        bld = False if obold < 0 else (bool(fc["flags"] & 18 or "bold" in fc["font"].lower()) or obold > 0)
                        sz = fc["size"] + osz
                        auto_center_x = ((r.x1 - r.x0) - (len(edit["replace"]) * sz * 0.55)) / 2.0

                        for step in range(max(1, obold + 1) if obold > 0 else 1):
                            pt = fitz.Point(fc["origin"][0] + auto_center_x + ox + (step * 0.15), fc["origin"][1] + oy)
                            inserts.append({"type": "text", "pt": pt, "txt": edit["replace"], "sz": sz, "bld": bld, "col": col, "ang": int(round(math.degrees(math.atan2(-fc["dir"][1], fc["dir"][0])) / 90.0) * 90) % 360, "fn": "telu" if tel else ("hebo" if bld else "helv"), "ff": FONT_TELUGU if tel and os.path.exists(FONT_TELUGU) else None})
                    continue
                else:
                    masks.append(fitz.Rect(rect.x0, rect.y0, rect.x1, rect.y1))
                    fs = float(edit.get("font_size", max(10.0, (rect.y1 - rect.y0) * 0.8))) + osz
                    bld = False if obold < 0 else (edit.get("is_bold", False) or obold > 0)
                    for step in range(max(1, obold + 1) if obold > 0 else 1):
                        pt = fitz.Point(rect.x0 + 2 + ox + (step * 0.15), rect.y1 - (fs * 0.2) + oy)
                        inserts.append({"type": "text", "pt": pt, "txt": edit["replace"], "sz": fs, "bld": bld, "col": col, "ang": edit.get("angle", 0), "fn": "helv", "ff": None})
                    continue

            masks.append(rect)
            if edit.get("type") == "image" and edit.get("replace_image"):
                b64 = edit["replace_image"].split(",")[1] if "," in edit["replace_image"] else edit["replace_image"]
                img_rect = fitz.Rect(rect.x0 - (osz / 2) + ox, rect.y0 - (osz / 2) + oy, rect.x1 + (osz / 2) + ox, rect.y1 + (osz / 2) + oy)
                inserts.append({"type": "image", "rect": img_rect, "bytes": base64.b64decode(b64)})
            elif edit.get("type") == "text" and edit.get("replace", ""):
                fs, ang = float(edit.get("font_size", 12.0)) + osz, int(edit.get("angle", 0))
                bld = False if obold < 0 else (edit.get("is_bold", False) or obold > 0)
                for step in range(max(1, obold + 1) if obold > 0 else 1):
                    if ang == 0: pt = fitz.Point(rect.x0 + 1 + ox + (step * 0.15), rect.y1 - (fs * 0.22) + oy)
                    elif ang == 90: pt = fitz.Point(rect.x0 + (fs * 0.78) + ox, rect.y1 - 1 + oy + (step * 0.15))
                    elif ang == 180: pt = fitz.Point(rect.x1 - 1 + ox - (step * 0.15), rect.y0 + (fs * 0.22) + oy)
                    elif ang == 270: pt = fitz.Point(rect.x1 - (fs * 0.22) + ox, rect.y0 + 1 + oy - (step * 0.15))
                    else: pt = fitz.Point(rect.x0 + 1 + ox + (step * 0.15), rect.y1 - (fs * 0.22) + oy)
                    tel = contains_telugu(edit["replace"])
                    inserts.append({"type": "text", "pt": pt, "txt": edit["replace"], "sz": fs, "bld": bld, "col": col, "ang": ang, "fn": "telu" if tel else ("hebo" if bld else "helv"), "ff": FONT_TELUGU if tel and os.path.exists(FONT_TELUGU) else None})

    elif mode == "auto":
        char_list = extract_character_sequence(page)
        if not char_list:
            pix = page.get_pixmap(dpi=150)
            img_arr = np.frombuffer(pix.samples, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(pix.h, pix.w, pix.n)
            bgr = cv2.cvtColor(img_arr, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR) if pix.n == 3 else cv2.cvtColor(img_arr, cv2.COLOR_BGRA2BGR)
            data, ocr_scale = get_ocr_data(bgr)
            pdf_scale = 72.0 / 150.0
            for edit in edits:
                if not (target := edit["search"].strip()): continue
                for i in range(len(data["text"])):
                    w_str = data["text"][i].strip()
                    if not w_str or float(data["conf"][i]) < 15.0: continue
                    if target == w_str.strip(string.punctuation):
                        x0, y0 = int(data["left"][i] * ocr_scale * pdf_scale), int(data["top"][i] * ocr_scale * pdf_scale)
                        x1, y1 = x0 + int(data["width"][i] * ocr_scale * pdf_scale), y0 + int(data["height"][i] * ocr_scale * pdf_scale)
                        masks.append(fitz.Rect(x0 - 1.5, y0 - 1.5, x1 + 1.5, y1 + 1.5))
                        if edit["replace"]:
                            roi_bgr = bgr[int(y0 / pdf_scale) : int(y1 / pdf_scale), int(x0 / pdf_scale) : int(x1 / pdf_scale)]
                            extracted_color = extract_dynamic_color(roi_bgr)
                            tel = contains_telugu(edit["replace"])
                            inserts.append({"type": "text", "pt": fitz.Point(x0, y1 - ((y1 - y0) * 0.2)), "txt": edit["replace"], "sz": (y1 - y0) * 0.8, "bld": False, "col": extracted_color, "ang": 0, "fn": "telu" if tel else "helv", "ff": FONT_TELUGU if tel and os.path.exists(FONT_TELUGU) else None})
        else:
            for edit in edits:
                for match in find_matches_via_matrix(char_list, edit["search"]):
                    r, fc = match["rect"], match["first_char"]
                    masks.append(fitz.Rect(r.x0 - 1.5, r.y0 - 1.5, r.x1 + 1.5, r.y1 + 1.5))
                    if edit["replace"]:
                        bld = bool(fc["flags"] & 18 or "bold" in fc["font"].lower() or "black" in fc["font"].lower())
                        c = (((fc["color"] >> 16) & 255) / 255.0, ((fc["color"] >> 8) & 255) / 255.0, (fc["color"] & 255) / 255.0)
                        auto_center_x = ((r.x1 - r.x0) - (len(edit["replace"]) * fc["size"] * 0.55)) / 2.0
                        tel = contains_telugu(edit["replace"])
                        inserts.append({"type": "text", "pt": fitz.Point(fc["origin"][0] + auto_center_x, fc["origin"][1]), "txt": edit["replace"], "sz": fc["size"], "bld": bld, "col": c if sum(c) <= 2.5 else (0.0, 0.0, 0.0), "ang": int(round(math.degrees(math.atan2(-fc["dir"][1], fc["dir"][0])) / 90.0) * 90) % 360, "fn": "telu" if tel else ("hebo" if bld else "helv"), "ff": FONT_TELUGU if tel and os.path.exists(FONT_TELUGU) else None})

    for m in masks:
        clip = m
        clip.intersect(page.rect)
        pix = page.get_pixmap(clip=clip, colorspace=fitz.csRGB, dpi=300)
        if pix.w > 0 and pix.h > 0:
            roi_bgr = cv2.cvtColor(np.frombuffer(pix.samples, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(pix.h, pix.w, pix.n), cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)
            ok, encoded = cv2.imencode(".png", inpaint_image_patch(roi_bgr))
            if ok:
                page.insert_image(clip, stream=bytes(encoded), overlay=True)
                
    for i in inserts:
        if i["type"] == "text":
            page.insert_text(i["pt"], i["txt"], fontsize=i["sz"], fontname=i["fn"], fontfile=i["ff"], color=i["col"], rotate=i["ang"], overlay=True)
        elif i["type"] == "image":
            page.insert_image(i["rect"], stream=i["bytes"], overlay=True)

    out = io.BytesIO()
    doc.save(out, garbage=4, deflate=True)
    doc.close()
    out.seek(0)
    return out.getvalue()


def apply_image_edits(image_bgr: np.ndarray, edits: list, mode: str) -> np.ndarray:
    mod = image_bgr.copy()

    def apply_single_edit(img, x0, y0, x1, y1, edit):
        pad = 2
        ox, oy = int(edit.get("offset_x", 0)), int(edit.get("offset_y", 0))
        osz, obold = int(edit.get("offset_size", 0)), int(edit.get("offset_bold", 0))

        y0_p, y1_p = max(0, int(y0) - pad), min(img.shape[0], int(y1) + pad)
        x0_p, x1_p = max(0, int(x0) - pad), min(img.shape[1], int(x1) + pad)
        roi = img[y0_p:y1_p, x0_p:x1_p]

        if roi.size > 0:
            img[y0_p:y1_p, x0_p:x1_p] = inpaint_image_patch(roi)

        if edit.get("type") == "image" and edit.get("replace_image"):
            b64 = edit["replace_image"].split(",")[1] if "," in edit["replace_image"] else edit["replace_image"]
            new_img = cv2.imdecode(np.frombuffer(base64.b64decode(b64), np.uint8), cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
            if new_img is not None:
                nx0, ny0 = int(x0 - (osz / 2) + ox), int(y0 - (osz / 2) + oy)
                nx1, ny1 = int(x1 + (osz / 2) + ox), int(y1 + (osz / 2) + oy)
                resized_img = cv2.resize(new_img, (max(1, nx1 - nx0), max(1, ny1 - ny0)))
                y_start, y_end = max(0, ny0), min(img.shape[0], ny1)
                x_start, x_end = max(0, nx0), min(img.shape[1], nx1)
                if y_start < y_end and x_start < x_end:
                    img[y_start:y_end, x_start:x_end] = resized_img[y_start - ny0 : y_start - ny0 + (y_end - y_start), x_start - nx0 : x_start - nx0 + (x_end - x_start)]
        elif edit.get("replace", ""):
            rep = edit["replace"]
            fs = int(edit.get("font_size", max(10, int((y1 - y0) * 0.88)))) + osz
            tel = contains_telugu(rep)
            try:
                font_file = FONT_TELUGU if tel and os.path.exists(FONT_TELUGU) else ("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf" if edit.get("is_bold", False) else "/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf")
                font = ImageFont.truetype(font_file, fs)
            except Exception:
                font = ImageFont.load_default()
                
            col_bgr = (int(edit["color"][2] * 255), int(edit["color"][1] * 255), int(edit["color"][0] * 255)) if "color" in edit else (0, 0, 0)
            text_mask = Image.new("L", (img.shape[1], img.shape[0]), 0)
            draw_mask = ImageDraw.Draw(text_mask)
            bbox = draw_mask.textbbox((0, 0), rep, font=font)
            text_x = x0 + ((x1 - x0) - (bbox[2] - bbox[0])) / 2 + ox
            text_y = y0 + ((y1 - y0) - (bbox[3] - bbox[1])) / 2 - (fs * 0.1) + oy
            draw_mask.text((text_x, text_y), rep, font=font, fill=255)
            mask_arr = np.array(text_mask)
            
            if obold > 0:
                mask_arr = cv2.dilate(mask_arr, cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE, (obold + 1, obold + 1)), iterations=1)
                
            text_opacity = { -1: 0.95, -2: 0.85, -3: 0.75, -4: 0.65 }.get(obold, 1.0)
            mask_float = (mask_arr.astype(np.float32) / 255.0) * text_opacity
            for c in range(3):
                img[:, :, c] = img[:, :, c] * (1 - mask_float) + col_bgr[c] * mask_float
        return img

    if mode == "manual":
        for edit in edits:
            x0, y0, x1, y1 = [int(v) for v in edit["box"]]
            search_str = edit.get("search", "").strip()

            if search_str and edit.get("type") == "text":
                roi_bgr = mod[max(0, y0):min(mod.shape[0], y1), max(0, x0):min(mod.shape[1], x1)]
                data, scale = get_ocr_data(roi_bgr)
                match_found = False
                for i in range(len(data["text"])):
                    w_str = data["text"][i].strip()
                    if not w_str or float(data["conf"][i]) < 15.0: continue
                    if search_str == w_str.strip(string.punctuation):
                        sx0, sy0 = max(0, x0) + int(data["left"][i] * scale), max(0, y0) + int(data["top"][i] * scale)
                        sx1, sy1 = sx0 + int(data["width"][i] * scale), sy0 + int(data["height"][i] * scale)
                        sub_edit = edit.copy()
                        sub_edit["box"] = [sx0, sy0, sx1, sy1]
                        mod = apply_single_edit(mod, sx0, sy0, sx1, sy1, sub_edit)
                        match_found = True
                if not match_found: mod = apply_single_edit(mod, x0, y0, x1, y1, edit)
            else: mod = apply_single_edit(mod, x0, y0, x1, y1, edit)
    elif mode == "auto":
        data, scale = get_ocr_data(mod)
        for edit in edits:
            if not (target := edit["search"].strip()): continue
            for i in range(len(data["text"])):
                w_str = data["text"][i].strip()
                if not w_str or float(data["conf"][i]) < 15.0: continue
                if target == w_str.strip(string.punctuation):
                    x0, y0 = int(data["left"][i] * scale), int(data["top"][i] * scale)
                    x1, y1 = x0 + int(data["width"][i] * scale), y0 + int(data["height"][i] * scale)
                    edit["color"] = extract_dynamic_color(mod[max(0, y0):min(mod.shape[0], y1), max(0, x0):min(mod.shape[1], x1)])
                    mod = apply_single_edit(mod, x0, y0, x1, y1, edit)
    return mod


if __name__ == "__main__":
    app.run(host="0.0.0.0", port=int(os.environ.get("PORT", 3000)), debug=False)

# =========================
# Builder
# =========================
FROM python:3.10-slim AS builder

WORKDIR /app

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    gcc \
    g++ \
    build-essential \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY requirements.txt .

RUN python -m venv /opt/venv

ENV PATH="/opt/venv/bin:$PATH"

RUN pip install --upgrade pip && \
    pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

# =========================
# Runtime
# =========================
FROM python:3.10-slim

WORKDIR /usr/src/app

RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    libgl1 \
    libglib2.0-0 \
    tesseract-ocr \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY --from=builder /opt/venv /opt/venv

ENV PATH="/opt/venv/bin:$PATH"

COPY . .

RUN useradd -m appuser && \
    chown -R appuser:appuser /usr/src/app

USER appuser

EXPOSE 3000

CMD ["python","app.py"]



========================================
FILE: ./README.md
========================================
# PixelEdit v2

A Flask + OpenCV + Tesseract + PyMuPDF editor for real text replacement.

## What changed

- Supports PDF and common image formats.
- Images are edited at the pixel level using OCR + OpenCV inpainting.
- Replacement text is redrawn into the edited image.
- JPG/JPEG, PNG, WEBP, BMP and TIFF output keeps the same image format.
- PDFs remain PDFs.
- Native PDF text uses PyMuPDF redaction/content editing.
- Scanned/image-only PDF pages can be OCR edited after rendering.
- Modern responsive UI with drag/drop, progress animation and result preview.
- `deploy.sh` is intentionally not included/changed.

## Run with Docker

```bash
docker build -t srinuas/pdfrepo:latest .
docker run -d --name pdfapp -p 3000:3000 srinuas/pdfrepo:latest
```

Then open `http://localhost:3000`.

## Important

OCR-based editing is strongest on clear, horizontal text with reasonable contrast. For arbitrary decorative fonts, handwriting, curved text, complex backgrounds or text overlapping graphics, pixel-perfect reconstruction cannot be guaranteed by OCR alone.

# encrypt .env file:

gpg -c .env

# You'll be prompted: 
# Enter passphrase: s
# Repeat passphrase: s

shred -u .env #removing more securely

# To display contents without creating a file:
gpg -d .env.gpg

# Restore Back to .env
gpg -d .env.gpg > .env

# result
cat .env

# Re-encrypt After Changes
vi .env
gpg -c .env
shred -u .env

# Verify Encryption
cat .env.gpg

# Stop the container:
docker stop app

# Remove the container:
docker rm app

# Remove a specific image (e.g., srinuas/pdfimg:1):
docker rmi srinuas/pdfimg:1

# Remove the Python slim image:
docker rmi python:3.10-slim

# Force stop and remove the container in one go:
docker rm -f app

# Clean up everything unused (Deletes all stopped containers and dangling images):
docker system prune -a


# for build and deploy

export GP='s' && docker rm -f app && docker build --no-cache -t srinuas/pdfimg:1 . && gpg --batch --yes --passphrase "$GP" -d .env.gpg > .env && docker run -itd --name app -p 333:3000 --env-file .env -v /root/srinu/pdf_edit:/usr/src/app srinuas/pdfimg:1

docker restart app

const $ = (s) => document.querySelector(s);
const $$ = (s) => document.querySelectorAll(s);

let session = { id: null, nativeWidth: 0, nativeHeight: 0 };
let currentMode = "auto";
let manualType = "text";
let manualBoxNative = null;
let suggestedFontSize = 12;
let manualTextProps = { angle: 0, is_bold: false, color: [0, 0, 0] };
let manualReplaceImageBase64 = null;
let currentZoom = 1;
let currentPage = 1;
let totalPages = 1;
let currentAdminFiles = []; // Holds fetched files for sorting

const fileInput = $("#fileInput");
const uploadSection = $("#uploadSection");
const editSection = $("#editSection");
const previewImg = $("#previewImg");
const drawCanvas = $("#drawCanvas");
const canvasContainer = $("#canvasContainer");
const canvasViewport = $("#canvasViewport");
const emptyState = $("#emptyState");
const undoBtn = $("#undoBtn");
const redoBtn = $("#redoBtn");
const newEditBtn = $("#newEditBtn");

let currentBg = "bg_img_1.jpg";

function setBackground(imgName) {
  if (currentBg === imgName && $("#appBackground").style.backgroundImage) return;
  currentBg = imgName;
  const bgDiv = document.getElementById("appBackground");
  bgDiv.style.opacity = 0;
  setTimeout(() => {
    bgDiv.style.backgroundImage = `url('${imgName}')`;
    bgDiv.style.opacity = 1;
  }, 500);
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  setBackground("bg_img_1.jpg");
});

window.showCustomAlert = function (message, title = "Notice") {
  $("#alertTitle").textContent = title;
  $("#alertMessage").textContent = message;
  $("#alertModal").classList.remove("hidden");
};

window.closeCustomAlert = function () {
  $("#alertModal").classList.add("hidden");
};

window.showToast = function (msg) {
  const toast = $("#toastNotification");
  toast.textContent = msg;
  toast.classList.remove("hidden");
  toast.style.opacity = 1;
  setTimeout(() => {
    toast.style.opacity = 0;
    setTimeout(() => toast.classList.add("hidden"), 300);
  }, 6000);
};

window.setZoom = function (step) {
  currentZoom = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(4.0, currentZoom + step));
  canvasContainer.style.transform = `scale(${currentZoom})`;
};

window.resetZoom = function () {
  currentZoom = 1;
  canvasContainer.style.transform = `scale(1)`;
};

window.scrollWindow = function(dir) {
  const step = 300;
  if (dir === 'up') window.scrollBy({ top: -step, behavior: 'smooth' });
  if (dir === 'down') window.scrollBy({ top: step, behavior: 'smooth' });
  if (dir === 'left') window.scrollBy({ left: -step, behavior: 'smooth' });
  if (dir === 'right') window.scrollBy({ left: step, behavior: 'smooth' });
};

window.scrollCanvas = function(dir) {
  const vp = document.getElementById('canvasViewport');
  const step = 200;
  if (dir === 'up') vp.scrollBy({ top: -step, behavior: 'smooth' });
  if (dir === 'down') vp.scrollBy({ top: step, behavior: 'smooth' });
  if (dir === 'left') vp.scrollBy({ left: -step, behavior: 'smooth' });
  if (dir === 'right') vp.scrollBy({ left: step, behavior: 'smooth' });
};

window.changePage = async function(action) {
  if (!session.id) return;
  const res = await fetch("/api/page", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ session_id: session.id, action: action })
  });
  if (res.ok) {
    const data = await res.json();
    currentPage = data.page;
    totalPages = data.total_pages;
    session.nativeWidth = data.width || session.nativeWidth;
    session.nativeHeight = data.height || session.nativeHeight;
    $("#pageCounter").textContent = `${currentPage} / ${totalPages}`;
    loadPreview();
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, drawCanvas.width, drawCanvas.height);
    cancelPreview();
  }
};

$("#btnAutoMode").onclick = () => switchMode("auto");
$("#btnManualMode").onclick = () => switchMode("manual");

function switchMode(mode) {
  currentMode = mode;
  $$(".mode-btn").forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
  if (mode === "auto") {
    $("#btnAutoMode").classList.add("active");
    $("#autoUI").classList.remove("hidden");
    $("#manualUI").classList.add("hidden");
    drawCanvas.style.pointerEvents = "none";
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, drawCanvas.width, drawCanvas.height);
    setBackground("bg_img_1.jpg");
  } else {
    $("#btnManualMode").classList.add("active");
    $("#manualUI").classList.remove("hidden");
    $("#autoUI").classList.add("hidden");
    drawCanvas.style.pointerEvents = "auto";
    setBackground("bg_img_2.jpg");
  }
}

window.setManualTab = function (type) {
  manualType = type;
  $$(".manual-tab").forEach((b) => b.classList.remove("active"));
  if (type === "text") {
    $("#tabText").classList.add("active");
    $("#manualTextInput").classList.remove("hidden");
    $("#manualImageInput").classList.add("hidden");
    $("#previewEditBtn").textContent = "1. Preview Text Replace";
  } else {
    $("#tabImage").classList.add("active");
    $("#manualImageInput").classList.remove("hidden");
    $("#manualTextInput").classList.add("hidden");
    $("#previewEditBtn").textContent = "1. Preview Image Replace";
  }
};

$("#manualReplaceImage").addEventListener("change", (e) => {
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (ev) => {
    manualReplaceImageBase64 = ev.target.result;
    $("#imageSelectName").textContent = file.name;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
});

function clearFile() {
  session.id = null;
  fileInput.value = "";
  uploadSection.classList.remove("hidden");
  editSection.classList.add("hidden");
  canvasViewport.classList.add("hidden");
  emptyState.classList.remove("hidden");
  newEditBtn.classList.add("hidden");
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, drawCanvas.width, drawCanvas.height);
  cancelPreview();
  resetZoom();
}

fileInput.addEventListener("change", async (e) => {
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;

  const fd = new FormData();
  fd.append("file", file);
  const res = await fetch("/api/upload", { method: "POST", body: fd });
  const data = await res.json();

  session.id = data.session_id;
  session.nativeWidth = data.width;
  session.nativeHeight = data.height;
  currentPage = data.current_page;
  totalPages = data.total_pages;

  $("#pageCounter").textContent = `${currentPage} / ${totalPages}`;

  uploadSection.classList.add("hidden");
  editSection.classList.remove("hidden");
  emptyState.classList.add("hidden");
  canvasViewport.classList.remove("hidden");
  newEditBtn.classList.add("hidden");

  updateHistoryButtons(false, false);
  loadPreview();
});

function loadPreview() {
  previewImg.src = `/api/preview/${session.id}?t=${Date.now()}`;
  previewImg.onload = () => {
    drawCanvas.width = previewImg.clientWidth;
    drawCanvas.height = previewImg.clientHeight;
    resetZoom();
  };
}

const ctx = drawCanvas.getContext("2d");
let isDrawing = false, startX, startY, currentX, currentY;

function getCoords(clientX, clientY) {
  const rect = drawCanvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const scaleX = drawCanvas.width / rect.width;
  const scaleY = drawCanvas.height / rect.height;
  return { x: (clientX - rect.left) * scaleX, y: (clientY - rect.top) * scaleY };
}

function startDrawing(clientX, clientY) {
  if (currentMode !== "manual") return;
  const pos = getCoords(clientX, clientY);
  startX = pos.x;
  startY = pos.y;
  isDrawing = true;
}

function drawLine(clientX, clientY) {
  if (!isDrawing) return;
  const pos = getCoords(clientX, clientY);
  currentX = pos.x;
  currentY = pos.y;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, drawCanvas.width, drawCanvas.height);
  ctx.strokeStyle = "#ff3366";
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 51, 102, 0.2)";
  ctx.fillRect(startX, startY, currentX - startX, currentY - startY);
  ctx.strokeRect(startX, startY, currentX - startX, currentY - startY);
}

async function stopDrawing() {
  if (!isDrawing) return;
  isDrawing = false;
  const scaleXNative = session.nativeWidth / drawCanvas.width;
  const scaleYNative = session.nativeHeight / drawCanvas.height;

  manualBoxNative = [
    Math.min(startX, currentX) * scaleXNative,
    Math.min(startY, currentY) * scaleYNative,
    Math.max(startX, currentX) * scaleXNative,
    Math.max(startY, currentY) * scaleYNative,
  ];

  $("#manualForm").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#nudgeBlock").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#scanResults").textContent = "Scanning formatting...";

  const res = await fetch("/api/extract", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ session_id: session.id, box: manualBoxNative }),
  });
  const data = await res.json();
  
  suggestedFontSize = data.suggested_font_size || 12;
  manualTextProps.angle = data.angle || 0;
  manualTextProps.is_bold = data.is_bold || false;
  manualTextProps.color = data.color || [0, 0, 0];

  const toHex = (c) => {
    let hex = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(c * 255))).toString(16);
    return hex.length === 1 ? "0" + hex : hex;
  };
  const hexColor = `#${toHex(manualTextProps.color[0])}${toHex(manualTextProps.color[1])}${toHex(manualTextProps.color[2])}`;

  $("#manualTextColor").value = hexColor;
  $("#originalColorSwatch").dataset.color = hexColor;
  $("#originalColorSwatch").style.background = hexColor;

  let words = data.lines.reduce((acc, l) => acc + l.word_count, 0);
  $("#scanResults").innerHTML = `<b>Box Linked.</b> Original text detected as: ${manualTextProps.angle}° Rotation ${manualTextProps.is_bold ? "(Bold)" : ""}.<br><small>${data.lines.map((l) => l.text).join(" ")}</small>`;
}

drawCanvas.addEventListener("mousedown", (e) => startDrawing(e.clientX, e.clientY));
drawCanvas.addEventListener("mousemove", (e) => drawLine(e.clientX, e.clientY));
drawCanvas.addEventListener("mouseup", stopDrawing);
drawCanvas.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => { if (isDrawing) stopDrawing(); });

drawCanvas.addEventListener("touchstart", (e) => {
  if (e.touches.length === 1) {
    e.preventDefault();
    startDrawing(e.touches[0].clientX, e.touches[0].clientY);
  }
}, { passive: false });

drawCanvas.addEventListener("touchmove", (e) => {
  if (e.touches.length === 1) {
    e.preventDefault();
    drawLine(e.touches[0].clientX, e.touches[0].clientY);
  }
}, { passive: false });

drawCanvas.addEventListener("touchend", () => { if (isDrawing) stopDrawing(); });

function updateHistoryButtons(canUndo, canRedo) {
  undoBtn.disabled = !canUndo;
  redoBtn.disabled = !canRedo;
}

async function triggerHistory(action) {
  const res = await fetch("/api/history", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ session_id: session.id, action: action }),
  });
  if (res.ok) {
    const data = await res.json();
    updateHistoryButtons(data.can_undo, data.can_redo);
    loadPreview();
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, drawCanvas.width, drawCanvas.height);
  }
}

undoBtn.onclick = () => triggerHistory("undo");
redoBtn.onclick = () => triggerHistory("redo");

window.nudgePos = function (dx, dy) {
  const xInput = $("#manualOffsetX"), yInput = $("#manualOffsetY");
  xInput.value = parseFloat(xInput.value || 0) + dx;
  yInput.value = parseFloat(yInput.value || 0) + dy;
  submitEdit("manual", true);
};

window.nudgeSize = function (ds) {
  const sizeInput = $("#manualOffsetSize");
  sizeInput.value = parseFloat(sizeInput.value || 0) + ds;
  submitEdit("manual", true);
};

let boldLevel = 0;
window.nudgeBold = function (db) {
  boldLevel = Math.max(-4, Math.min(4, boldLevel + db));
  $("#manualOffsetBold").value = boldLevel;
  $("#boldLevelDisplay").textContent = `Weight: ${boldLevel === 0 ? "Normal" : boldLevel > 0 ? `B+${boldLevel}` : `B${boldLevel}`}`;
  submitEdit("manual", true);
};

window.setColor = function (hex) {
  $("#manualTextColor").value = hex;
  submitEdit("manual", true);
};

$("#manualTextColor").addEventListener("input", () => submitEdit("manual", true));

window.cancelPreview = function () {
  loadPreview();
  $("#manualForm").classList.add("hidden");
  $("#nudgeBlock").classList.add("hidden");
  $("#manualSearchInput").value = "";
  $("#manualReplaceInput").value = "";
  $("#manualOffsetX").value = "0";
  $("#manualOffsetY").value = "0";
  $("#manualOffsetSize").value = "0";
  $("#manualOffsetBold").value = "0";
  boldLevel = 0;
  $("#boldLevelDisplay").textContent = `Weight: Normal`;
  manualReplaceImageBase64 = null;
  $("#imageSelectName").textContent = "Choose Image...";
  manualBoxNative = null;
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, drawCanvas.width, drawCanvas.height);
};

window.submitEdit = async function (mode, isPreview = false) {
  let edits = [];

  if (mode === "auto") {
    edits = [...$$(".edit-row")].map((row) => ({
      search: row.querySelector(".search-input").value.trim(),
      replace: row.querySelector(".replace-input").value,
    })).filter((x) => x.search);
  } else if (mode === "manual") {
    if (!manualBoxNative) return showCustomAlert("Draw a box first.", "Selection Required");

    const hex = $("#manualTextColor").value;
    const customColor = [
      parseInt(hex.substring(1, 3), 16) / 255,
      parseInt(hex.substring(3, 5), 16) / 255,
      parseInt(hex.substring(5, 7), 16) / 255,
    ];

    if (manualType === "text") {
      edits = [{
        type: "text", box: manualBoxNative,
        search: $("#manualSearchInput").value, replace: $("#manualReplaceInput").value,
        offset_x: parseFloat($("#manualOffsetX").value) || 0,
        offset_y: parseFloat($("#manualOffsetY").value) || 0,
        offset_size: parseFloat($("#manualOffsetSize").value) || 0,
        offset_bold: parseInt($("#manualOffsetBold").value) || 0,
        font_size: suggestedFontSize, angle: manualTextProps.angle,
        is_bold: manualTextProps.is_bold, color: customColor,
      }];
    } else {
      if (!manualReplaceImageBase64) return showCustomAlert("Please select an image first.", "Image Required");
      edits = [{
        type: "image", box: manualBoxNative, replace_image: manualReplaceImageBase64,
        offset_x: parseFloat($("#manualOffsetX").value) || 0,
        offset_y: parseFloat($("#manualOffsetY").value) || 0,
        offset_size: parseFloat($("#manualOffsetSize").value) || 0,
      }];
    }
  }

  const res = await fetch("/api/edit", {
    method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ session_id: session.id, mode: mode, edits: edits, is_preview: isPreview }),
  });

  if (res.ok) {
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data.status === "preview") {
      $("#previewImg").src = "data:image/png;base64," + data.image;
      $("#previewEditBtn").classList.add("hidden");
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, drawCanvas.width, drawCanvas.height);
    } else {
      updateHistoryButtons(data.can_undo, data.can_redo);
      loadPreview();
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, drawCanvas.width, drawCanvas.height);
      $("#manualForm").classList.add("hidden");
      $("#nudgeBlock").classList.add("hidden");
      $("#previewEditBtn").classList.remove("hidden");
      $("#manualSearchInput").value = "";
      $("#manualReplaceInput").value = "";
      $("#manualOffsetX").value = "0";
      $("#manualOffsetY").value = "0";
      $("#manualOffsetSize").value = "0";
      $("#manualOffsetBold").value = "0";
      boldLevel = 0;
      $("#boldLevelDisplay").textContent = `Weight: Normal`;
      manualReplaceImageBase64 = null;
      $("#imageSelectName").textContent = "Choose Image...";
      manualBoxNative = null;
      showToast("Changes Applied Successfully!");
    }
  } else {
    showCustomAlert("Editing failed. Please try again.", "Error");
  }
};

function downloadFile() {
  window.location.href = `/api/download/${session.id}`;
  setTimeout(() => { newEditBtn.classList.remove("hidden"); }, 1500);
}

function addEditRow() {
  const row = document.createElement("div");
  row.className = "edit-row slide-in";
  row.draggable = true;
  row.innerHTML = `
    <div class="drag-handle">⠿</div>
    <div class="edit-fields">
      <input class="search-input" type="text" placeholder="Find">
      <button class="swap-btn" type="button">⇄</button>
      <input class="replace-input" type="text" placeholder="Replace">
    </div>
    <button class="remove-btn" type="button">×</button>
  `;
  row.querySelector(".remove-btn").onclick = () => row.remove();
  row.querySelector(".swap-btn").onclick = () => {
    const sInput = row.querySelector(".search-input"), rInput = row.querySelector(".replace-input"), temp = sInput.value;
    sInput.value = rInput.value; rInput.value = temp;
  };
  row.addEventListener("dragstart", () => row.classList.add("dragging-row"));
  row.addEventListener("dragend", () => row.classList.remove("dragging-row"));
  $("#edits").appendChild(row);
}

$("#edits").addEventListener("dragover", (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const afterElement = [...$("#edits").querySelectorAll(".edit-row:not(.dragging-row)")].reduce(
    (closest, child) => {
      const box = child.getBoundingClientRect();
      const offset = e.clientY - box.top - box.height / 2;
      return offset < 0 && offset > closest.offset ? { offset: offset, element: child } : closest;
    }, { offset: Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY }
  ).element;

  const draggable = document.querySelector(".dragging-row");
  if (draggable) afterElement == null ? $("#edits").appendChild(draggable) : $("#edits").insertBefore(draggable, afterElement);
});
$("#addBtn").onclick = addEditRow;
addEditRow();

function openAdmin() {
  $("#pinModal").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#pinInput").value = "";
  setTimeout(() => $("#pinInput").focus(), 100);
}
function closePinModal() { $("#pinModal").classList.add("hidden"); }

// Auto-submit when 4 digits are entered
$("#pinInput").addEventListener("input", (e) => {
  if (e.target.value.length === 4) submitPin();
});
// Keep Enter key fallback
$("#pinInput").addEventListener("keypress", (e) => { if (e.key === "Enter") submitPin(); });

async function submitPin() {
  const pin = $("#pinInput").value;
  if (!pin) return;
  const btn = $("#submitPinBtn");
  const origText = btn.textContent;
  btn.textContent = "Verifying...";
  btn.disabled = true;

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/verify", {
      method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ pin: pin }),
    });

    if (res.ok) {
      closePinModal();
      $("#adminModal").classList.remove("hidden");
      setBackground("bg_img_3.jpg");
      await loadAdminTab("Uploaded", "Images");
    } else {
      const content = $(".pin-content");
      content.classList.remove("shake");
      void content.offsetWidth;
      content.classList.add("shake");
      $("#pinInput").value = "";
      $("#pinInput").focus();
      showCustomAlert("Incorrect PIN. Please try again.", "Access Denied");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showCustomAlert("Network error. Could not verify PIN.", "Error");
  } finally {
    btn.textContent = origText;
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
}

let vaultUnlocked = false;

window.toggleTrashView = function () {
  if (vaultUnlocked) {
    // If unlocked, clicking the icon hides the tabs and locks the vault again
    vaultUnlocked = false;
    $$(".trash-tab").forEach((tab) => tab.classList.add("hidden"));
    if (adminState.folder === "Deleted") loadAdminTab("Uploaded", "Images");
  } else {
    // If locked, prompt for PIN immediately upon clicking the 🐦‍🔥 icon
    $("#vaultPinModal").classList.remove("hidden");
    $("#vaultPinInput").value = "";
    setTimeout(() => $("#vaultPinInput").focus(), 100);
  }
};

function closeAdmin() {
  fetch("/api/admin/logout", { method: "POST" });
  $("#adminModal").classList.add("hidden");
  $$(".trash-tab").forEach((tab) => tab.classList.add("hidden"));
  vaultUnlocked = false; // Lock vault completely on exit
  setBackground(currentMode === "auto" ? "bg_img_1.jpg" : "bg_img_2.jpg");
  if (adminState.folder === "Deleted") loadAdminTab("Uploaded", "Images");
}

function closeVaultPinModal() {
  $("#vaultPinModal").classList.add("hidden");
}

// Auto-submit Vault PIN instantly when 4 digits are entered
$("#vaultPinInput").addEventListener("input", (e) => {
  if (e.target.value.length === 4) submitVaultPin();
});
$("#vaultPinInput").addEventListener("keypress", (e) => { if (e.key === "Enter") submitVaultPin(); });

async function submitVaultPin() {
  const pin = $("#vaultPinInput").value;
  if (!pin) return;
  const btn = $("#submitVaultPinBtn");
  const origText = btn.textContent;
  btn.textContent = "Verifying...";
  btn.disabled = true;

  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/verify_vault", {
      method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ pin: pin }),
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      vaultUnlocked = true;
      closeVaultPinModal();
      // Only reveal the hidden Deleted tabs upon successful PIN
      $$(".trash-tab").forEach((tab) => tab.classList.remove("hidden"));
    } else {
      const content = $("#vaultPinModal .pin-content");
      content.classList.remove("shake");
      void content.offsetWidth;
      content.classList.add("shake");
      $("#vaultPinInput").value = "";
      $("#vaultPinInput").focus();
      showCustomAlert("Incorrect Vault PIN. Please try again.", "Access Denied");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    showCustomAlert("Network error.", "Error");
  } finally {
    btn.textContent = origText;
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
}

window.loadAdminTab = async function (folder, type) {
  // Relock Vault automatically if user clicks away to normal Uploaded/Downloaded tabs
  if (folder !== "Deleted") {
    vaultUnlocked = false;
    $$(".trash-tab").forEach((tab) => tab.classList.add("hidden"));
  }

  adminState = { folder, type };

  $$(".admin-tab").forEach((b) => {
    b.classList.remove("active");
    if (b.textContent.trim() === `${folder} ${type}`) b.classList.add("active");
  });

  if (folder === "Deleted") {
    $("#adminTrashBtn").classList.add("hidden");
    $("#adminPermDeleteBtn").classList.remove("hidden");
  } else {
    $("#adminTrashBtn").classList.remove("hidden");
    $("#adminPermDeleteBtn").classList.add("hidden");
  }

  $("#adminGrid").innerHTML = "";
  $("#adminLoader").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#sortSelect").value = "date-desc"; // Reset Sort

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/list?folder=${folder}&type=${type}`);
    if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {
      $("#adminLoader").classList.add("hidden");
      return showCustomAlert("Unauthorized access.", "Access Denied");
    }

    const files = await res.json();
    $("#adminLoader").classList.add("hidden");
    if (files.error) return showCustomAlert("Admin API Error: " + files.error, "API Error");
    
    currentAdminFiles = files; // Store files to allow sorting locally
    window.renderAdminGrid(); // Render with sorting
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Dashboard Error:", error);
    $("#adminLoader").classList.add("hidden");
  }
};

window.renderAdminGrid = function() {
  const grid = $("#adminGrid");
  if (!currentAdminFiles || currentAdminFiles.length === 0) {
    grid.innerHTML = "<div class='empty-admin'>No files found in this directory.</div>";
    return;
  }

  const sortType = $("#sortSelect").value;
  
  // Regex finds any 14 digit sequence (YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS) anywhere in the name
  const getTimestamp = (name) => {
    const match = name.match(/(\d{8}_\d{6})/);
    return match ? match[1] : "";
  };

  let sortedFiles = [...currentAdminFiles];
  sortedFiles.sort((a, b) => {
    if (sortType === "name-asc") return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
    if (sortType === "name-desc") return b.name.localeCompare(a.name);
    
    const timeA = getTimestamp(a.name);
    const timeB = getTimestamp(b.name);
    
    if (sortType === "date-desc") {
      if (timeA && timeB) return timeB.localeCompare(timeA);
      return b.name.localeCompare(a.name); // Fallback if no timestamp
    }
    if (sortType === "date-asc") {
      if (timeA && timeB) return timeA.localeCompare(timeB);
      return a.name.localeCompare(b.name); // Fallback if no timestamp
    }
    return 0;
  });

  grid.innerHTML = "";
  const isImg = adminState.type === "Images";

  sortedFiles.forEach((f) => {
    const item = document.createElement("div");
    item.className = "admin-file-card fade-in";
    item.innerHTML = `
      <input type="checkbox" class="admin-chk" data-path="${f.path}" data-sha="${f.sha}" data-url="${f.url}" data-name="${f.name}">
      <div class="file-visual">
          ${isImg ? `<img src="${f.url}" loading="lazy">` : `<div class="pdf-icon">PDF</div>`}
          <div class="file-actions">
              <button class="action-hover-btn" onclick="previewFile(event, '${f.url}', ${isImg}, '${f.name}')" title="Preview File">👁️</button>
              <button class="action-hover-btn" onclick="downloadSingleFile(event, '${f.url}', '${f.name}')" title="Download File">⬇️</button>
          </div>
      </div>
      <div class="file-name" title="${f.name}">${f.name}</div>
    `;
    item.onclick = (e) => {
      if (e.target.tagName !== "INPUT" && !e.target.classList.contains("action-hover-btn")) {
        const chk = item.querySelector("input");
        chk.checked = !chk.checked;
      }
    };
    grid.appendChild(item);
  });
};

window.downloadSingleFile = async function (e, url, name) {
  e.stopPropagation();
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Network response was not ok");
    const blob = await response.blob();
    const blobUrl = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.style.display = "none";
    a.href = blobUrl;
    a.download = name;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
    document.body.removeChild(a);
  } catch (err) {
    showCustomAlert("Failed to download file.", "Download Error");
  }
};

window.downloadSelected = async function () {
  const selected = [...$$(".admin-chk:checked")];
  if (selected.length === 0) return showCustomAlert("Select at least one file to download.", "Selection Required");
  $("#adminLoader").classList.remove("hidden");
  for (let i = 0; i < selected.length; i++) {
    await downloadSingleFile({ stopPropagation: () => {} }, selected[i].dataset.url, selected[i].dataset.name);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
  }
  $("#adminLoader").classList.add("hidden");
  showToast("Downloads complete.");
};

window.previewFile = async function (e, url, isImg, name) {
  e.stopPropagation();
  setBackground("bg_img_4.jpg");
  $("#previewTitle").textContent = name;
  $("#filePreviewModal").classList.remove("hidden");
  const container = $("#previewContainer");

  if (isImg) {
    container.innerHTML = `<img src="${url}" style="max-width: 100%; max-height: 100%; object-fit: contain; border-radius: 8px;">`;
  } else {
    container.innerHTML = '<div class="loader"></div>';
    try {
      const response = await fetch(url);
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Failed to load");
      const rawBlob = await response.blob();
      const pdfBlob = new Blob([rawBlob], { type: "application/pdf" });
      if (currentBlobUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(currentBlobUrl);
      currentBlobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(pdfBlob);
      container.innerHTML = `<iframe src="${currentBlobUrl}#toolbar=0&navpanes=0" style="width: 100%; height: 100%; border: none; border-radius: 8px; background: white;"></iframe>`;
    } catch (err) {
      container.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align: center; color: #94a3b8;">
        <div style="font-size: 40px; margin-bottom: 10px;">📄</div>
        <p>Browser preview unavailable.</p>
        <a href="${url}" target="_blank" class="primary-btn" style="text-decoration: none; display: inline-block; padding: 10px 20px; margin-top: 15px;">Download PDF Directly</a>
      </div>`;
    }
  }
};

window.closeFilePreview = function () {
  $("#filePreviewModal").classList.add("hidden");
  $("#previewContainer").innerHTML = "";
  if (currentBlobUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(currentBlobUrl); currentBlobUrl = null; }
  setBackground($("#adminModal").classList.contains("hidden") ? (currentMode === "auto" ? "bg_img_1.jpg" : "bg_img_2.jpg") : "bg_img_3.jpg");
};

window.selectAll = function (state) { $$(".admin-chk").forEach((chk) => (chk.checked = state)); };

window.executeAdminAction = async function (action) {
  const selected = [...$$(".admin-chk:checked")].map((chk) => ({ path: chk.dataset.path, sha: chk.dataset.sha }));
  if (selected.length === 0) return showCustomAlert("Select at least one file.", "Selection Required");

  if (action === "delete") {
    const confirmed = await new Promise((resolve) => {
      $("#confirmModal").classList.remove("hidden");
      $("#confirmYesBtn").onclick = () => { $("#confirmModal").classList.add("hidden"); resolve(true); };
      $("#confirmCancelBtn").onclick = () => { $("#confirmModal").classList.add("hidden"); resolve(false); };
    });
    if (!confirmed) return;
  }

  $("#adminLoader").classList.remove("hidden");
  try {
    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/action", {
      method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ action: action, files: selected }),
    });
    if (res.ok) await loadAdminTab(adminState.folder, adminState.type);
    else { showCustomAlert("Action failed or unauthorized.", "Action Failed"); $("#adminLoader").classList.add("hidden"); }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Action Error:", error);
    showCustomAlert("Network error occurred.", "Network Error");
    $("#adminLoader").classList.add("hidden");
  }
};

#!/bin/bash

set -e

# ==============================
# Configuration
# ==============================
# BRANCH="main4"

DOCKER_USER="srinuas"
REPO_NAME="pdfrepo"
TAG="latest"

IMAGE_NAME="${DOCKER_USER}/${REPO_NAME}:${TAG}"
CONTAINER_NAME="pdfapp"

HOST_PORT="333"
CONTAINER_PORT="3000"

echo "======================================"
echo " PDF Editor - Build & Deploy"
echo "======================================"
echo "Branch    : ${BRANCH}"
echo "Image     : ${IMAGE_NAME}"
echo "Container : ${CONTAINER_NAME}"
echo "Port      : ${HOST_PORT}:${CONTAINER_PORT}"
echo "======================================"

# --------------------------------------
# Build image
# --------------------------------------
echo "[3/6] Building Docker image..."

docker build -t "${IMAGE_NAME}" .

# --------------------------------------
# Push image
# --------------------------------------
echo "[4/6] Pushing image to Docker Hub..."

# docker push "${IMAGE_NAME}"

# --------------------------------------
# Stop old container
# --------------------------------------
echo "[5/6] Replacing old container..."

docker rm -f "${CONTAINER_NAME}" 2>/dev/null || true

# --------------------------------------
# Start new container
# --------------------------------------
echo "[6/6] Starting new container..."

docker run -d \
    --name "${CONTAINER_NAME}" \
    --restart unless-stopped \
    -p "${HOST_PORT}:${CONTAINER_PORT}" \
    "${IMAGE_NAME}"

echo ""
echo "======================================"
echo " Deployment completed successfully"
echo "======================================"

echo "Container:"
docker ps --filter "name=${CONTAINER_NAME}"

echo ""
echo "Application:"
echo "http://3.143.235.117:${HOST_PORT}"

echo ""
echo "Docker image:"
echo "${IMAGE_NAME}"

echo "======================================"

FROM python:3.11-slim

ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 \
    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
    PIP_NO_CACHE_DIR=1

WORKDIR /usr/src/app

RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
       tesseract-ocr \
       tesseract-ocr-eng \
       tesseract-ocr-tel \
       tesseract-ocr-hin \
       fonts-dejavu \
       fonts-noto-core \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt

COPY . .

EXPOSE 3000
CMD ["python", "app.py"]
========================================
FILE: ./README.md
========================================
# PixelEdit v2

A Flask + OpenCV + Tesseract + PyMuPDF editor for real text replacement.

## What changed

- Supports PDF and common image formats.
- Images are edited at the pixel level using OCR + OpenCV inpainting.
- Replacement text is redrawn into the edited image.
- JPG/JPEG, PNG, WEBP, BMP and TIFF output keeps the same image format.
- PDFs remain PDFs.
- Native PDF text uses PyMuPDF redaction/content editing.
- Scanned/image-only PDF pages can be OCR edited after rendering.
- Modern responsive UI with drag/drop, progress animation and result preview.
- `deploy.sh` is intentionally not included/changed.

## Run with Docker

```bash
docker build -t srinuas/pdfrepo:latest .
docker run -d --name pdfapp -p 3000:3000 srinuas/pdfrepo:latest
```

Then open `http://localhost:3000`.

## Important

OCR-based editing is strongest on clear, horizontal text with reasonable contrast. For arbitrary decorative fonts, handwriting, curved text, complex backgrounds or text overlapping graphics, pixel-perfect reconstruction cannot be guaranteed by OCR alone.

# encrypt .env file:

gpg -c .env

# You'll be prompted: 
# Enter passphrase: s
# Repeat passphrase: s

shred -u .env #removing more securely

# To display contents without creating a file:
gpg -d .env.gpg

# Restore Back to .env
gpg -d .env.gpg > .env

# result
cat .env

# Re-encrypt After Changes
vi .env
gpg -c .env
shred -u .env

# Verify Encryption
cat .env.gpg

# Stop the container:
docker stop app

# Remove the container:
docker rm app

# Remove a specific image (e.g., srinuas/pdfimg:1):
docker rmi srinuas/pdfimg:1

# Remove the Python slim image:
docker rmi python:3.10-slim

# Force stop and remove the container in one go:
docker rm -f app

# Clean up everything unused (Deletes all stopped containers and dangling images):
docker system prune -a


# for build and deploy with Bind Mount Volume:
export GP='s' && docker rm -f app && docker build --no-cache -t srinuas/pdfimg:1 . && gpg --batch --yes --passphrase "$GP" -d .env.gpg > .env && docker run -itd --name app -p 333:3000 --env-file .env -v /root/srinu/pdf_edit:/usr/src/app srinuas/pdfimg:1

docker restart app

# Format the Python file (app.py)
black app.py

# Format all front-end files (HTML, CSS, JS) instantly using Node's runner
npx prettier --write "public/index.html" "public/style.css" "public/script.js"
